## Contradictions

- DOJ press release: 'charge over $7 billion in fees each year' and complaint para. 64: 'over $7 billion in network fees on U.S. debit volume annually' · versus · Same DOJ press release, closing paragraph: 'Visa charges roughly $8 billion in network fees on U.S. debit volume annually' · https://www.justice.gov/opa/pr/justice-department-sues-visa-monopolizing-debit-markets · https://www.justice.gov/opa/media/1370421/dl?inline
- Payments Dive 2026-08-20 and 2026-08-10: fact discovery closes October 16, 2026 (per Jan. 22, 2026 scheduling order) · versus · Docket entry 203 (Sept. 3, 2026) amended fact discovery deadline to January 29, 2027; the press dates predate the amendment · https://www.paymentsdive.com/news/visa-doj-lawyers-clash-ahead-of-trial/828378/ · https://www.courtlistener.com/docket/69190163/united-states-v-visa-inc/
- RBA Consultation Paper (July 2025) Option 4 expected card networks to submit a plan 'by September 2026' · versus · RBA Conclusions Paper (March 2026) instead expects published Scheme Fee Roadmaps by 1 April 2027 · https://www.rba.gov.au/payments-and-infrastructure/review-of-retail-payments-regulation/2026-03/conclusions-paper/scheme-fees.html · https://www.rba.gov.au/payments-and-infrastructure/review-of-retail-payments-regulation/2026-03/conclusions-paper/scheme-fees.html
- RBA reports a card network saying MSF data show competition is functioning effectively · versus · PSB concludes scheme fees, particularly for credit, are not subject to effective competitive constraints · https://www.rba.gov.au/payments-and-infrastructure/review-of-retail-payments-regulation/2026-03/conclusions-paper/scheme-fees.html · https://www.rba.gov.au/payments-and-infrastructure/review-of-retail-payments-regulation/2026-03/conclusions-paper/scheme-fees.html
- Task brief states Maestro retirement runs off through 2027. · versus · Mastercard FY2025 10-K (2026-02-11) still lists Maestro as an active brand with no phase-out language, and the Fed reports Maestro 2024 US debit interchange data; the only fetched retirement source (Wikipedia) gives July 1, 2023 as the Europe-only start of the phase-out and no end date. · https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1141391/000114139126000013/ma-20251231.htm · https://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Maestro_(debit_card)
- Mastercard 10-K (Feb 2026): November 2025 revised settlement 'if approved by the court, would resolve the litigation'; briefing on preliminary approval completed. · versus · Digital Transactions (n.d., on Sep 25, 2026 home page): 'Merchants Level Multiple Objections to a Proposed Interchange Agreement' and 'A Deal Looks No Closer'. · https://www.sec.gov/Archives/edgar/data/1141391/000114139126000013/ma-20251231.htm · https://www.digitaltransactions.net/
